<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="ne">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>CLVA Retention Console</title>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/xlsx/0.18.5/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>
<style>
  :root{
    --bg:#F6F7FB;
    --surface:#FFFFFF;
    --surface-2:#F0F2F8;
    --surface-3:#E6E9F3;
    --border:#DFE3EF;
    --border-soft:#E9ECF5;
    --text:#171B2C;
    --text-dim:#5B6480;
    --text-faint:#9198B5;
    --green:#0E9F6E;
    --green-dim:#DDF6EA;
    --amber:#C2760A;
    --amber-dim:#FBEDD3;
    --red:#D7263D;
    --red-dim:#FBE1E4;
    --cyan:#0E7AAE;
    --cyan-dim:#DFF1F9;
    --font-display: -apple-system, "Segoe UI", "Helvetica Neue", Arial, sans-serif;
    --font-mono: ui-monospace, "SF Mono", "Cascadia Mono", "Roboto Mono", Consolas, monospace;
  }
  *{box-sizing:border-box; margin:0; padding:0;}
  body{
    background:
      radial-gradient(1200px 600px at 15% -10%, #EDF0FA 0%, transparent 60%),
      radial-gradient(1000px 500px at 100% 0%, #E7F5F1 0%, transparent 55%),
      var(--bg);
    color:var(--text);
    font-family:var(--font-display);
    min-height:100vh;
    padding-bottom:80px;
  }
  ::selection{ background:var(--cyan); color:#FFFFFF; }

  /* ---------- Header ---------- */
  header{
    padding:28px clamp(16px,4vw,48px) 20px;
    border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-soft);
    display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:flex-end; gap:16px; flex-wrap:wrap;
  }
  .brand-eyebrow{
    font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:11px; letter-spacing:.18em; text-transform:uppercase;
    color:var(--cyan); margin-bottom:8px; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;
  }
  .brand-eyebrow .dot{ width:6px; height:6px; border-radius:50%; background:var(--green); box-shadow:0 0 8px var(--green); animation:pulse 2s infinite; }
  @keyframes pulse{ 0%,100%{opacity:1;} 50%{opacity:.35;} }
  h1{ font-size:clamp(22px,3vw,30px); font-weight:700; letter-spacing:-0.01em; }
  .subtitle{ color:var(--text-dim); font-size:13.5px; margin-top:6px; max-width:640px; line-height:1.5;}
  .header-stats{ display:flex; gap:26px; font-family:var(--font-mono); }
  .header-stats div{ text-align:right; }
  .header-stats .num{ font-size:22px; font-weight:600; color:var(--text);}
  .header-stats .lbl{ font-size:10.5px; color:var(--text-faint); text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.08em; margin-top:2px;}

  /* ---------- Layout ---------- */
  main{ padding:24px clamp(16px,4vw,48px); display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:34px; max-width:1400px; margin:0 auto; }
  section{ scroll-margin-top:100px; }
  .section-head{ display:flex; align-items:baseline; gap:12px; margin-bottom:16px; flex-wrap:wrap;}
  .section-head .idx{ font-family:var(--font-mono); color:var(--text-faint); font-size:13px; }
  .section-head h2{ font-size:17px; font-weight:700; letter-spacing:-.01em; }
  .section-head .desc{ color:var(--text-dim); font-size:12.5px; }

  /* ---------- Filter bar ---------- */
  .filterbar{
    position:sticky; top:0; z-index:40;
    background:rgba(246,247,251,.9); backdrop-filter:blur(10px);
    border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-soft);
    padding:14px clamp(16px,4vw,48px);
    display:flex; align-items:center; gap:14px; flex-wrap:wrap;
  }
  .filterbar label, .toolbar label{ font-size:10.5px; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.08em; color:var(--text-faint); display:block; margin-bottom:4px; font-family:var(--font-mono);}
  .filter-group{ display:flex; flex-direction:column; min-width:180px; }
  select, input[type=text]{
    background:var(--surface-2); border:1px solid var(--border); color:var(--text);
    padding:8px 10px; border-radius:8px; font-size:13px; font-family:var(--font-display);
    outline:none;
  }
  select:focus, input:focus{ border-color:var(--cyan); }
  .btn{
    background:var(--surface-2); border:1px solid var(--border); color:var(--text);
    padding:9px 14px; border-radius:8px; font-size:12.5px; cursor:pointer; font-family:var(--font-display);
    display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:7px; font-weight:600; transition:border-color .15s, background .15s;
  }
  .btn:hover{ border-color:var(--cyan); background:var(--surface-3); }
  .btn.primary{ background:var(--cyan); color:#FFFFFF; border-color:var(--cyan); }
  .btn.primary:hover{ filter:brightness(1.08); }
  .btn.ghost{ background:transparent; }
  .filter-tag{
    margin-left:auto; font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:12px; color:var(--text-dim);
    display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px;
  }
  .filter-tag b{ color:var(--cyan); font-weight:600;}

  /* ---------- Overview cards ---------- */
  .grid-4{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr); gap:14px; }
  @media (max-width:900px){ .grid-4{ grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);} }
  @media (max-width:560px){ .grid-4{ grid-template-columns:1fr;} }
  .grid-2{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr); gap:14px; margin-top:14px; }
  @media (max-width:700px){ .grid-2{ grid-template-columns:1fr;} }
  .stat-card.ratio .value{ display:flex; align-items:baseline; gap:10px; }
  .stat-card.ratio .value .pctnum{ font-size:32px; }
  .stat-card.ratio .value .fraction{ font-size:14px; color:var(--text-faint); font-weight:500; }
  .ratio-track{ width:100%; height:6px; background:var(--surface-3); border-radius:4px; overflow:hidden; margin-top:10px;}
  .ratio-fill{ height:100%; border-radius:4px; transition:width .3s; }
  .stat-card{
    background:var(--surface); border:1px solid var(--border-soft); border-radius:14px; padding:18px 18px 16px;
    position:relative; overflow:hidden;
  }
  .stat-card::before{
    content:""; position:absolute; top:0; left:0; width:3px; height:100%; background:var(--accent, var(--cyan));
  }
  .stat-card .top-row{ display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:flex-start; }
  .stat-card .lbl{ font-size:11.5px; color:var(--text-dim); text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.06em; }
  .stat-card .icon{ font-size:16px; opacity:.7; }
  .stat-card .value{ font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:32px; font-weight:700; margin-top:10px; letter-spacing:-.02em; }
  .stat-card .foot{ font-size:12px; color:var(--text-faint); margin-top:8px; line-height:1.5; }
  .stat-card .foot b{ color:var(--text-dim); }
  .stat-card .foot.warn{ color:var(--amber); }
  .stat-card a.inline-upload{ color:var(--cyan); cursor:pointer; text-decoration:underline; text-underline-offset:2px;}

  /* ---------- Upload zone ---------- */
  .upload-grid{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr; gap:14px;}
  @media (max-width:800px){ .upload-grid{ grid-template-columns:1fr;} }
  .upload-card{
    border:1.5px dashed var(--border); background:var(--surface); border-radius:14px; padding:18px;
    display:flex; align-items:center; gap:14px;
  }
  .upload-card.loaded{ border-style:solid; border-color:var(--green); background:linear-gradient(180deg,var(--green-dim),var(--surface) 70%); }
  .upload-card .uic{ font-size:22px; width:42px; height:42px; border-radius:10px; background:var(--surface-2); display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; flex-shrink:0;}
  .upload-card .ut{ font-weight:700; font-size:13.5px; }
  .upload-card .ud{ color:var(--text-dim); font-size:12px; margin-top:3px; line-height:1.5;}
  .upload-card .ustatus{ font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:11.5px; color:var(--green); margin-top:6px;}
  .upload-card .actions{ margin-left:auto; display:flex; flex-direction:column; gap:6px; align-items:flex-end;}
  .upload-card input[type=file]{ display:none; }

  /* ---------- Summary Analysis ---------- */
  .focus-grid{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr; gap:14px; margin-bottom:16px;}
  @media (max-width:800px){ .focus-grid{ grid-template-columns:1fr;} }
  .focus-card{ background:var(--surface); border:1px solid var(--border-soft); border-radius:14px; padding:16px 18px;}
  .focus-card h3{ font-size:12.5px; text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.07em; display:flex; align-items:center; gap:7px; margin-bottom:12px;}
  .focus-card.need h3{ color:var(--red); }
  .focus-card.best h3{ color:var(--green); }
  .focus-row{ display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; padding:9px 0; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-soft); font-size:13px;}
  .focus-row:last-child{ border-bottom:none; }
  .focus-row .fname{ font-weight:600; }
  .focus-row .fsub{ color:var(--text-faint); font-size:11px; font-family:var(--font-mono); margin-top:2px;}
  .focus-row .fmetric{ font-family:var(--font-mono); font-weight:700; text-align:right; }
  .focus-row .fmetric small{ display:block; font-weight:400; color:var(--text-faint); font-size:10px;}

  table{ width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; font-size:12.5px; }
  thead th{
    text-align:left; padding:10px 12px; color:var(--text-faint); font-weight:600; text-transform:uppercase;
    font-size:10.5px; letter-spacing:.05em; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); position:sticky; top:0; background:var(--surface);
    cursor:pointer; user-select:none; white-space:nowrap;
  }
  thead th:hover{ color:var(--cyan); }
  thead th .arrow{ opacity:.4; font-size:9px; margin-left:3px;}
  tbody td{ padding:9px 12px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border-soft); font-family:var(--font-mono); white-space:nowrap; }
  tbody tr:hover{ background:var(--surface-2); }
  .table-wrap{ background:var(--surface); border:1px solid var(--border-soft); border-radius:14px; overflow:hidden; }
  .table-scroll{ max-height:460px; overflow:auto; }
  .pill{ display:inline-block; padding:3px 9px; border-radius:99px; font-size:10.5px; font-weight:700; font-family:var(--font-mono); letter-spacing:.02em;}
  .pill.red{ background:var(--red-dim); color:var(--red); }
  .pill.amber{ background:var(--amber-dim); color:var(--amber); }
  .pill.green{ background:var(--green-dim); color:var(--green); }
  .bar-track{ width:70px; height:5px; background:var(--surface-3); border-radius:3px; overflow:hidden; display:inline-block; vertical-align:middle;}
  .bar-fill{ height:100%; border-radius:3px; }

  /* ---------- Follow-up ---------- */
  .fu-grid{ display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(5,1fr); gap:12px; margin-bottom:16px;}
  @media (max-width:900px){ .fu-grid{ grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);} }
  .fu-card{ background:var(--surface); border:1px solid var(--border-soft); border-radius:12px; padding:14px; text-align:center;}
  .fu-card .fv{ font-family:var(--font-mono); font-size:26px; font-weight:700; }
  .fu-card .fl{ font-size:10.5px; color:var(--text-faint); text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.06em; margin-top:4px;}
  #fuTable th, #fuTable td{ text-align:center; }
  #fuTable th:nth-child(1), #fuTable td:nth-child(1),
  #fuTable th:nth-child(2), #fuTable td:nth-child(2){
    text-align:left; position:sticky; left:0; background:var(--surface); z-index:5;
  }
  #fuTable th:nth-child(2), #fuTable td:nth-child(2){ left:120px; }
  #fuTable th:last-child, #fuTable td:last-child{ position:sticky; right:0; background:var(--surface-2); font-weight:700; z-index:5; }

  .empty-note{ padding:30px; text-align:center; color:var(--text-faint); font-size:13px; }
  .empty-note b{ color:var(--cyan); cursor:pointer; text-decoration:underline; }

  .toolbar{ display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; gap:12px; margin-bottom:12px; flex-wrap:wrap;}
  .search-box{ min-width:220px; }
  .footer-note{ text-align:center; color:var(--text-faint); font-size:11.5px; margin-top:10px; font-family:var(--font-mono);}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<header>
  <div>
    <div class="brand-eyebrow"><span class="dot"></span> Region 5 &middot; CLVA Module 2</div>
    <h1>Retention Console — High Risk Renewals</h1>
    <div class="subtitle">Habitual &amp; one-time-late renewers ranked by churn risk, tracked from first alert through billing and field follow-up.</div>
  </div>
  <div class="header-stats">
    <div><div class="num" id="hdrBranches">–</div><div class="lbl">Branches</div></div>
    <div><div class="num" id="hdrOlts">–</div><div class="lbl">OLTs</div></div>
    <div><div class="num" id="hdrTotal">–</div><div class="lbl">Users loaded</div></div>
  </div>
</header>

<div class="filterbar">
  <div class="filter-group">
    <label>Branch</label>
    <select id="branchFilter"></select>
  </div>
  <div class="filter-group">
    <label>OLT</label>
    <select id="oltFilter"></select>
  </div>
  <button class="btn ghost" id="resetFilters">↺ Reset filters</button>
  <div class="filter-tag" id="filterSummary"></div>
</div>

<main>

  <!-- 1. OVERVIEW -->
  <section id="overview">
    <div class="section-head"><span class="idx">01</span><h2>Overview</h2><span class="desc">— current filter scope</span></div>
    <div class="grid-4">
      <div class="stat-card" style="--accent:var(--red)">
        <div class="top-row"><span class="lbl">High-risk users</span><span class="icon">⚠</span></div>
        <div class="value" id="statHighRisk">–</div>
        <div class="foot">Flagged on the CLVA M2 risk list, all risk scores <b>70+</b>.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stat-card" style="--accent:var(--amber)">
        <div class="top-row"><span class="lbl">Still counting to expiry</span><span class="icon">⏳</span></div>
        <div class="value" id="statCountdown">–</div>
        <div class="foot" id="statCountdownFoot">—</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stat-card" style="--accent:var(--green)" id="cardBilled">
        <div class="top-row"><span class="lbl">Already billed</span><span class="icon">✓</span></div>
        <div class="value" id="statBilled">–</div>
        <div class="foot" id="statBilledFoot">Upload the payment behaviour sheet below to match renewals by username.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stat-card" style="--accent:var(--red)">
        <div class="top-row"><span class="lbl">Expired &amp; unpaid</span><span class="icon">✕</span></div>
        <div class="value" id="statUnpaid">–</div>
        <div class="foot" id="statUnpaidFoot">Past expiry date with no matching renewal invoice found.</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="grid-2">
      <div class="stat-card ratio" style="--accent:var(--green)">
        <div class="top-row"><span class="lbl">HR Bill %</span><span class="icon">💳</span></div>
        <div class="value"><span class="pctnum" id="statHrBillPct">–</span><span class="fraction" id="statHrBillFrac"></span></div>
        <div class="ratio-track"><div class="ratio-fill" id="statHrBillBar" style="width:0%;background:var(--green)"></div></div>
        <div class="foot" id="statHrBillFoot">Matched billing users ÷ users already past expiry in this scope.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="stat-card ratio" style="--accent:var(--cyan)">
        <div class="top-row"><span class="lbl">HR Follow-up %</span><span class="icon">📞</span></div>
        <div class="value"><span class="pctnum" id="statHrFuPct">–</span><span class="fraction" id="statHrFuFrac"></span></div>
        <div class="ratio-track"><div class="ratio-fill" id="statHrFuBar" style="width:0%;background:var(--cyan)"></div></div>
        <div class="foot" id="statHrFuFoot">Users with ≥1 follow-up call ÷ users already past expiry in this scope.</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="table-wrap" style="margin-top:14px;">
      <div class="section-head" style="margin:14px 14px 10px;">
        <h2 style="font-size:14px;">OLT-wise raw breakdown</h2>
        <span class="desc">— till today, per OLT</span>
      </div>
      <div class="table-scroll">
        <table id="oltRawTable">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th data-key="branch">Branch<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="olt">OLT<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="raw">Raw<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="tillExpiry">Till Day Expiry<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="tillBill">Till Day Bill<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="tillFu">Till Day Follow Up<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="billPct">Bill %<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="fuPct">Follow Up %<span class="arrow"></span></th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="oltRawTableBody"></tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- 2. SUMMARY ANALYSIS -->
  <section id="summary">
    <div class="section-head"><span class="idx">02</span><h2>Summary analysis</h2><span class="desc">— OLT performance across the current scope</span></div>

    <div class="focus-grid">
      <div class="focus-card need">
        <h3>⚠ Needs focus</h3>
        <div id="needFocusList"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="focus-card best">
        <h3>↑ Best improving</h3>
        <div id="bestImprovingList"></div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="table-wrap">
      <div class="table-scroll">
        <table id="oltTable">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th data-key="branch">Branch<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="olt">OLT<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="users">Users<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="avgRisk">Avg risk<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="expired">Expired<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="billedPct">Billed %<span class="arrow"></span></th>
              <th data-key="trend">Trend (latest vs avg gap)<span class="arrow"></span></th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="oltTableBody"></tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- 2b. HIGH-RISK LIST -->
  <section id="riskdata">
    <div class="section-head"><span class="idx">02</span><h2>High-risk user list</h2><span class="desc">— the dataset the whole dashboard is built on</span></div>
    <div class="upload-grid">
      <label class="upload-card loaded" id="riskCard" for="riskInput">
        <div class="uic">🎯</div>
        <div>
          <div class="ut">High-risk user list</div>
          <div class="ud">Same format as the risk export (USERNAME, BRANCH, OLT, RISK SCORE, PRIORITY, ...). Uploading a new file replaces the currently loaded list below.</div>
          <div class="ustatus" id="riskStatus">✓ Built-in dataset loaded</div>
        </div>
        <div class="actions">
          <span class="btn primary">Upload .xlsx</span>
          <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="riskReset" style="display:none;">↺ Reset</button>
        </div>
        <input type="file" id="riskInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv">
      </label>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- 3. BILLING MATCH -->
  <section id="billing">
    <div class="section-head"><span class="idx">03</span><h2>Match billing data</h2><span class="desc">— upload the "Customer Payment Behaviour" export to see who has already renewed</span></div>
    <div class="upload-grid">
      <label class="upload-card" id="billingCard" for="billingInput">
        <div class="uic">💳</div>
        <div>
          <div class="ut">Payment behaviour sheet</div>
          <div class="ud">Same format as "Customer Payment Behaviour (days gap...)" — matched to the risk list by USERNAME.</div>
          <div class="ustatus" id="billingStatus"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="actions">
          <span class="btn primary">Upload .xlsx</span>
          <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="billingReset" style="display:none;">↺ Reset</button>
        </div>
        <input type="file" id="billingInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv">
      </label>
      <label class="upload-card" id="fuCard" for="fuInput">
        <div class="uic">📞</div>
        <div>
          <div class="ut">Follow-up (CFU) sheet</div>
          <div class="ud">Same format as the CFU follow-up export — matched by USERNAME, bucketed by days overdue at call time.</div>
          <div class="ustatus" id="fuStatus"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="actions">
          <span class="btn primary">Upload .xlsx</span>
          <button type="button" class="btn ghost" id="fuReset" style="display:none;">↺ Reset</button>
        </div>
        <input type="file" id="fuInput" accept=".xlsx,.xls,.csv">
      </label>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- 4. FOLLOW UP -->
  <section id="followup">
    <div class="section-head"><span class="idx">04</span><h2>Follow-up coverage</h2><span class="desc">— calls made per OLT, day by day</span></div>
    <div id="fuEmpty" class="empty-note">No follow-up sheet loaded yet. <b id="fuEmptyLink">Upload the CFU export above</b> to see day-wise coverage.</div>
    <div id="fuContent" style="display:none;">
      <div class="fu-grid">
        <div class="fu-card" style="--accent:var(--cyan)"><div class="fv" id="fuTotal">0</div><div class="fl">Total follow-ups</div></div>
        <div class="fu-card"><div class="fv" id="fuUsers">0</div><div class="fl">High-risk users reached</div></div>
        <div class="fu-card"><div class="fv" id="fuDays">0</div><div class="fl">Days covered</div></div>
        <div class="fu-card"><div class="fv" id="fuOlts">0</div><div class="fl">OLTs with activity</div></div>
        <div class="fu-card"><div class="fv" id="fuAvgDay">0</div><div class="fl">Avg calls / day</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="table-wrap">
        <div class="table-scroll" style="max-height:520px;">
          <table id="fuTable">
            <thead id="fuTableHead"></thead>
            <tbody id="fuTableBody"></tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="footer-note">Columns are the actual follow-up dates found in the uploaded CFU sheet, matched to high-risk usernames in the current filter scope.</div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <!-- 5. UNPAID LIST -->
  <section id="unpaidlist">
    <div class="section-head"><span class="idx">05</span><h2>Expired &amp; unpaid list</h2><span class="desc">— already past expiry, no renewal invoice matched</span></div>
    <div class="toolbar">
      <input type="text" id="unpaidSearch" class="search-box" placeholder="Search username, branch or OLT...">

</script>
<script>
/* ============================================================
   CLVA Retention Console — app logic
   RAW_COLUMNS / RAW_DATA are injected above this script.
   Columns: username, branch, olt, riskScore, priority, avgDiff,
            latestDiff, daysRemaining, expiryDate, revenue, type, status
   daysRemaining: positive = days left before expiry,
                  0 = expires today, negative = days overdue (disabled)
   ============================================================ */

const COLOR = { red:'#D7263D', amber:'#C2760A', green:'#0E9F6E', cyan:'#0E7AAE' };

// Convert raw arrays into objects
function buildAllUsersFromRaw(){
  return RAW_DATA.map(r => {
    const o = {};
    RAW_COLUMNS.forEach((c,i) => o[c] = r[i]);
    o.usernameKey = (o.username || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return o;
  });
}
let ALL_USERS = buildAllUsersFromRaw();

let billingMap = null;   // Map(usernameKey -> {amount, transDate, ...}) or null if not uploaded
let followupRows = null; // array of parsed followup rows, or null

/* ---------------- Filters ---------------- */
const branchSel = document.getElementById('branchFilter');
const oltSel = document.getElementById('oltFilter');

function uniqueSorted(arr){ return Array.from(new Set(arr.filter(Boolean))).sort(); }

function buildBranchOptions(){
  const branches = uniqueSorted(ALL_USERS.map(u => u.branch));
  branchSel.innerHTML = '<option value="__ALL__">All branches</option>' +
    branches.map(b => `<option value="${b}">${b}</option>`).join('');
}

function buildOltOptions(){
  const branch = branchSel.value;
  const pool = branch === '__ALL__' ? ALL_USERS : ALL_USERS.filter(u => u.branch === branch);
  const olts = uniqueSorted(pool.map(u => u.olt));
  oltSel.innerHTML = '<option value="__ALL__">All OLTs</option>' +
    olts.map(o => `<option value="${o}">${o}</option>`).join('');
}

branchSel.addEventListener('change', () => { buildOltOptions(); render(); });
oltSel.addEventListener('change', render);
document.getElementById('resetFilters').addEventListener('click', () => {
  branchSel.value = '__ALL__'; buildOltOptions(); oltSel.value = '__ALL__'; render();
});

function currentFiltered(){
  const b = branchSel.value, o = oltSel.value;
  return ALL_USERS.filter(u =>
    (b === '__ALL__' || u.branch === b) &&
    (o === '__ALL__' || u.olt === o)
  );
}

/* ---------------- Header ---------------- */
function renderHeader(){
  document.getElementById('hdrBranches').textContent = uniqueSorted(ALL_USERS.map(u=>u.branch)).length;
  document.getElementById('hdrOlts').textContent = uniqueSorted(ALL_USERS.map(u=>u.olt)).length;
  document.getElementById('hdrTotal').textContent = ALL_USERS.length.toLocaleString();
}

/* ---------------- Overview ---------------- */
function renderOverview(filtered){
  document.getElementById('statHighRisk').textContent = filtered.length.toLocaleString();

  const countdown = filtered.filter(u => u.daysRemaining !== null && u.daysRemaining > 0);
  const soon = countdown.filter(u => u.daysRemaining <= 7).length;
  document.getElementById('statCountdown').textContent = countdown.length.toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById('statCountdownFoot').innerHTML =
    `<b>${soon}</b> expire within 7 days.`;

  const expiredOrDisabled = filtered.filter(u => u.daysRemaining !== null && u.daysRemaining <= 0);

  if (billingMap){
    const billed = filtered.filter(u => billingMap.has(u.usernameKey));
    document.getElementById('statBilled').textContent = billed.length.toLocaleString();
    const pct = filtered.length ? ((billed.length/filtered.length)*100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';
    document.getElementById('statBilledFoot').innerHTML = `<b>${pct}%</b> of this scope has a matching renewal invoice.`;

    const unpaidExpired = expiredOrDisabled.filter(u => !billingMap.has(u.usernameKey));
    document.getElementById('statUnpaid').textContent = unpaidExpired.length.toLocaleString();
    document.getElementById('statUnpaidFoot').innerHTML = `Out of <b>${expiredOrDisabled.length}</b> already past expiry in this scope.`;
  } else {
    document.getElementById('statBilled').textContent = '—';
    document.getElementById('statBilledFoot').innerHTML = `Upload the payment behaviour sheet below to match renewals by username.`;
    document.getElementById('statUnpaid').textContent = expiredOrDisabled.length.toLocaleString();
    document.getElementById('statUnpaidFoot').innerHTML = `Past expiry / disabled in this scope. Upload billing data to exclude those already paid.`;
  }

  renderRatioCards(filtered);
}

function renderRatioCards(filtered){
  const total = filtered.length;
  const expired = filtered.filter(u => u.daysRemaining !== null && u.daysRemaining <= 0);
  const expiredTotal = expired.length;

  // HR Bill % = matched billing users among ALREADY EXPIRED users / total already-expired users
  const billPctEl = document.getElementById('statHrBillPct');
  const billFracEl = document.getElementById('statHrBillFrac');
  const billBarEl = document.getElementById('statHrBillBar');
  const billFootEl = document.getElementById('statHrBillFoot');
  if (billingMap){
    const billedCount = expired.filter(u => billingMap.has(u.usernameKey)).length;
    const pct = expiredTotal ? (billedCount/expiredTotal*100) : 0;
    billPctEl.textContent = pct.toFixed(1) + '%';
    billFracEl.textContent = `(${billedCount.toLocaleString()} / ${expiredTotal.toLocaleString()})`;
    billBarEl.style.width = Math.min(pct,100) + '%';
    billFootEl.innerHTML = `<b>${billedCount}</b> of <b>${expiredTotal}</b> already-expired users matched a renewal invoice.`;
  } else {
    billPctEl.textContent = '—';
    billFracEl.textContent = '';
    billBarEl.style.width = '0%';
    billFootEl.innerHTML = `Upload the payment behaviour sheet below to calculate this.`;
  }

  // HR Follow-up % = matched follow-up users among ALREADY EXPIRED users / total already-expired users
  const fuPctEl = document.getElementById('statHrFuPct');
  const fuFracEl = document.getElementById('statHrFuFrac');
  const fuBarEl = document.getElementById('statHrFuBar');
  const fuFootEl = document.getElementById('statHrFuFoot');
  if (followupRows){
    const fuKeySet = new Set(followupRows.map(f => f.usernameKey));
    const fuMatchedCount = expired.filter(u => fuKeySet.has(u.usernameKey)).length;
    const pct = expiredTotal ? (fuMatchedCount/expiredTotal*100) : 0;
    fuPctEl.textContent = pct.toFixed(1) + '%';
    fuFracEl.textContent = `(${fuMatchedCount.toLocaleString()} / ${expiredTotal.toLocaleString()})`;
    fuBarEl.style.width = Math.min(pct,100) + '%';
    fuFootEl.innerHTML = `<b>${fuMatchedCount}</b> of <b>${expiredTotal}</b> already-expired users received at least one follow-up call.`;
  } else {
    fuPctEl.textContent = '—';
    fuFracEl.textContent = '';
    fuBarEl.style.width = '0%';
    fuFootEl.innerHTML = `Upload the CFU follow-up sheet below to calculate this.`;
  }
}

/* ---------------- OLT-wise raw breakdown ---------------- */
let oltRawSortKey = 'raw', oltRawSortDir = -1;

function computeOltRawSummary(filtered){
  const groups = {};
  filtered.forEach(u => {
    const key = u.branch + '||' + u.olt;
    if (!groups[key]) groups[key] = { branch:u.branch, olt:u.olt, users:[] };
    groups[key].users.push(u);
  });
  const fuKeySet = followupRows ? new Set(followupRows.map(f => f.usernameKey)) : null;

  return Object.values(groups).map(g => {
    const raw = g.users.length;
    const expired = g.users.filter(u => u.daysRemaining !== null && u.daysRemaining <= 0);
    const tillExpiry = expired.length;
    const tillBill = billingMap ? expired.filter(u => billingMap.has(u.usernameKey)).length : null;
    const tillFu = fuKeySet ? expired.filter(u => fuKeySet.has(u.usernameKey)).length : null;
    const billPct = billingMap ? (tillExpiry ? (tillBill/tillExpiry*100) : 0) : null;
    const fuPct = fuKeySet ? (tillExpiry ? (tillFu/tillExpiry*100) : 0) : null;
    return { branch:g.branch, olt:g.olt, raw, tillExpiry, tillBill, tillFu, billPct, fuPct };
  });
}

function renderOltRawTable(filtered){
  const rows = computeOltRawSummary(filtered);
  rows.sort((a,b) => {
    const va = a[oltRawSortKey], vb = b[oltRawSortKey];
    if (va === null || va === undefined) return 1; if (vb === null || vb === undefined) return -1;
    if (typeof va === 'string') return va.localeCompare(vb) * oltRawSortDir;
    return (va - vb) * oltRawSortDir;
  });

  const tbody = document.getElementById('oltRawTableBody');
  tbody.innerHTML = rows.map(r => `
    <tr>
      <td style="color:var(--text-dim)">${r.branch}</td>
      <td><b>${r.olt}</b></td>
      <td>${r.raw}</td>
      <td>${r.tillExpiry > 0 ? `<span class="pill red">${r.tillExpiry}</span>` : '0'}</td>
      <td>${r.tillBill === null ? '<span style="color:var(--text-faint)">—</span>' : r.tillBill}</td>
      <td>${r.tillFu === null ? '<span style="color:var(--text-faint)">—</span>' : r.tillFu}</td>
      <td>${r.billPct === null ? '<span style="color:var(--text-faint)">upload data</span>' : billedBar(r.billPct)}</td>
      <td>${r.fuPct === null ? '<span style="color:var(--text-faint)">upload data</span>' : billedBar(r.fuPct)}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('') || `<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-faint);padding:24px;">No data in this scope.</td></tr>`;
}

document.querySelectorAll('#oltRawTable thead th').forEach(th => {
  th.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const key = th.dataset.key;
    if (oltRawSortKey === key) oltRawSortDir *= -1; else { oltRawSortKey = key; oltRawSortDir = -1; }
    render();
  });
});

/* ---------------- Summary analysis (OLT-wise) ---------------- */
let oltSortKey = 'users', oltSortDir = -1;

function computeOltSummary(filtered){
  const groups = {};
  filtered.forEach(u => {
    const key = u.branch + '||' + u.olt;
    if (!groups[key]) groups[key] = { branch:u.branch, olt:u.olt, users:[] };
    groups[key].users.push(u);
  });
  const fuByOlt = followupRows ? groupFollowupsByOlt(filtered) : null;

  return Object.values(groups).map(g => {
    const n = g.users.length;
    const avgRisk = g.users.reduce((s,u)=>s+(u.riskScore||0),0)/n;
    const expired = g.users.filter(u => u.daysRemaining !== null && u.daysRemaining <= 0).length;
    const billed = billingMap ? g.users.filter(u => billingMap.has(u.usernameKey)).length : null;
    const billedPct = billingMap ? (n ? (billed/n*100) : 0) : null;
    // trend: avg( latestDiff - avgDiff ). Positive => gap shrinking (improving), negative => worsening
    const trend = g.users.reduce((s,u)=> s + ((u.latestDiff ?? 0) - (u.avgDiff ?? 0)), 0) / n;
    const key = g.branch+'||'+g.olt;
    const fu = fuByOlt ? (fuByOlt[key] || 0) : null;
    return { branch:g.branch, olt:g.olt, users:n, avgRisk, expired, billedPct, trend, fu };
  });
}

function renderOltTable(filtered){
  const rows = computeOltSummary(filtered);
  rows.sort((a,b) => {
    const va = a[oltSortKey], vb = b[oltSortKey];
    if (va === null) return 1; if (vb === null) return -1;
    if (typeof va === 'string') return va.localeCompare(vb) * oltSortDir;
    return (va - vb) * oltSortDir;
  });

  const tbody = document.getElementById('oltTableBody');
  tbody.innerHTML = rows.map(r => `
    <tr>
      <td style="color:var(--text-dim)">${r.branch}</td>
      <td><b>${r.olt}</b></td>
      <td>${r.users}</td>
      <td>${riskPill(r.avgRisk)}</td>
      <td>${r.expired > 0 ? `<span class="pill red">${r.expired}</span>` : '0'}</td>
      <td>${r.billedPct === null ? '<span style="color:var(--text-faint)">upload data</span>' : billedBar(r.billedPct)}</td>
      <td>${trendCell(r.trend)}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('') || `<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-faint);padding:24px;">No data in this scope.</td></tr>`;

  // Focus panels
  renderFocusPanels(rows);
}

function riskPill(v){
  const cls = v >= 90 ? 'red' : v >= 80 ? 'amber' : 'green';
  return `<span class="pill ${cls}">${v.toFixed(1)}</span>`;
}
function billedBar(pct){
  const color = pct >= 60 ? COLOR.green : pct >= 30 ? COLOR.amber : COLOR.red;
  return `<span class="bar-track"><span class="bar-fill" style="width:${pct}%;background:${color}"></span></span> ${pct.toFixed(0)}%`;
}
function trendCell(t){
  const up = t > 2, down = t < -2;
  const color = up ? COLOR.green : down ? COLOR.red : 'var(--text-dim)';
  const arrow = up ? '▲' : down ? '▼' : '—';
  return `<span style="color:${color}">${arrow} ${t.toFixed(1)}d</span>`;
}

function renderFocusPanels(rows){
  const eligible = rows.filter(r => r.users >= 3);

  // Needs focus: high expired count + high avg risk, weighted score
  const needScored = eligible.map(r => ({...r, score: r.expired*2 + (r.avgRisk-70)*0.3 + (r.billedPct !== null ? (100-r.billedPct)*0.15 : 0)}))
    .sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,5);
  document.getElementById('needFocusList').innerHTML = needScored.length ? needScored.map(r => `
    <div class="focus-row">
      <div>
        <div class="fname">${r.olt}</div>
        <div class="fsub">${r.branch} · ${r.users} users</div>
      </div>
      <div class="fmetric" style="color:var(--red)">${r.expired} expired<small>avg risk ${r.avgRisk.toFixed(1)}</small></div>
    </div>
  `).join('') : `<div style="color:var(--text-faint);font-size:12.5px;padding:8px 0;">Not enough OLTs (need ≥3 users each) in this scope.</div>`;

  // Best improving: highest positive trend
  const bestScored = eligible.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.trend-a.trend).slice(0,5).filter(r=>r.trend > 0);
  document.getElementById('bestImprovingList').innerHTML = bestScored.length ? bestScored.map(r => `
    <div class="focus-row">
      <div>
        <div class="fname">${r.olt}</div>
        <div class="fsub">${r.branch} · ${r.users} users</div>
      </div>
      <div class="fmetric" style="color:var(--green)">▲ ${r.trend.toFixed(1)}d<small>gap narrowing</small></div>
    </div>
  `).join('') : `<div style="color:var(--text-faint);font-size:12.5px;padding:8px 0;">No OLT shows a narrowing payment gap in this scope yet.</div>`;
}

document.querySelectorAll('#oltTable thead th').forEach(th => {
  th.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const key = th.dataset.key;
    if (oltSortKey === key) oltSortDir *= -1; else { oltSortKey = key; oltSortDir = -1; }
    render();
  });
});

/* ---------------- High-risk list upload ---------------- */
document.getElementById('riskInput').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (ev) => {
    try {
      const wb = XLSX.read(ev.target.result, { type:'array', cellDates:true });
      const sheet = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
      const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, { header:1, defval:null });
      const headerRowIdx = rows.findIndex(r => r.some(c => String(c||'').trim().toUpperCase() === 'USERNAME'));
      if (headerRowIdx === -1){ alert('USERNAME column not found in this sheet.'); return; }
      const header = rows[headerRowIdx].map(h => String(h||'').trim().toUpperCase());
      const findCol = (...names) => {
        for (const n of names){ const idx = header.indexOf(n); if (idx >= 0) return idx; }
        return -1;
      };
      const uCol = findCol('USERNAME');
      const branchCol = findCol('BRANCH');
      const oltCol = findCol('OLT');
      const riskCol = findCol('RISK SCORE','RISKSCORE','RISK');
      const priorityCol = findCol('PRIORITY');
      const avgDiffCol = findCol('AVG DIFF','AVGDIFF');
      const latestDiffCol = findCol('LATEST DIFF','LATESTDIFF');
      const daysRemCol = findCol('DAYS REMAINING','DAYSREMAINING','DAYS REM');
      const expiryCol = findCol('EXPIRY DATE','EXPIRYDATE');
      const revenueCol = findCol('REVENUE','FORECAST REVENUE','FORECAST REVENUE (NPR)');
      const typeCol = findCol('TYPE');
      const statusCol = findCol('STATUS');

      const newUsers = [];
      for (let i = headerRowIdx+1; i < rows.length; i++){
        const row = rows[i];
        const uname = row[uCol];
        if (!uname) continue;
        newUsers.push({
          username: uname,
          usernameKey: String(uname).trim().toLowerCase(),
          branch: branchCol>=0 ? row[branchCol] : null,
          olt: oltCol>=0 ? row[oltCol] : null,
          riskScore: riskCol>=0 ? Number(row[riskCol]) : null,
          priority: priorityCol>=0 ? Number(row[priorityCol]) : null,
          avgDiff: avgDiffCol>=0 ? Number(row[avgDiffCol]) : null,
          latestDiff: latestDiffCol>=0 ? Number(row[latestDiffCol]) : null,
          daysRemaining: daysRemCol>=0 ? Number(row[daysRemCol]) : null,
          expiryDate: expiryCol>=0 ? row[expiryCol] : null,
          revenue: revenueCol>=0 ? Number(row[revenueCol]) : null,
          type: typeCol>=0 ? row[typeCol] : null,
          status: statusCol>=0 ? row[statusCol] : null
        });
      }
      if (!newUsers.length){ alert('No usable rows found in this sheet.'); return; }

      ALL_USERS = newUsers;
      document.getElementById('riskStatus').textContent = `✓ ${file.name} — ${ALL_USERS.length.toLocaleString()} users loaded`;
      document.getElementById('riskCard').classList.add('loaded');
      document.getElementById('riskReset').style.display = 'inline-flex';
      billingMap = null;
      followupRows = null;
      document.getElementById('billingStatus').textContent = '';
      document.getElementById('billingCard').classList.remove('loaded');
      document.getElementById('billingReset').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('billingInput').value = '';
      document.getElementById('fuStatus').textContent = '';
      document.getElementById('fuCard').classList.remove('loaded');
      document.getElementById('fuReset').style.display = 'none';
      document.getElementById('fuInput').value = '';
      if (window.storage){
        window.storage.delete('billing-data', false).catch(()=>{});
        window.storage.delete('followup-data', false).catch(()=>{});
      }
      saveRiskToStorage(file.name);
      branchSel.value = '__ALL__';
      buildBranchOptions();
      buildOltOptions();
      oltSel.value = '__ALL__';
      renderHeader();
      render();
    } catch(err){
      alert('Could not read this file: ' + err.message);
    }
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
});

document.getElementById('riskReset').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
  ALL_USERS = buildAllUsersFromRaw();
  document.getElementById('riskStatus').textContent = '✓ Built-in dataset loaded';
  document.getElementById('riskCard').classList.add('loaded');
  document.getElementById('riskReset').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('riskInput').value = '';
  if (window.storage){ window.storage.delete('risk-data', false).catch(()=>{}); }
  branchSel.value = '__ALL__';
  buildBranchOptions();
  buildOltOptions();
  oltSel.value = '__ALL__';
  renderHeader();
  render();
});

/* ---------------- Billing upload ---------------- */
document.getElementById('billingInput').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (ev) => {
    try {
      const wb = XLSX.read(ev.target.result, { type:'array', cellDates:true });
      const sheet = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
      const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, { header:1, defval:null });
      const headerRowIdx = rows.findIndex(r => r.some(c => String(c||'').trim().toUpperCase() === 'USERNAME'));
      if (headerRowIdx === -1){ alert('USERNAME column not found in this sheet.'); return; }
      const header = rows[headerRowIdx].map(h => String(h||'').trim().toUpperCase());
      const uCol = header.indexOf('USERNAME');
      const amtCol = header.indexOf('AMOUNT');
      const dateCol = header.indexOf('RECENT INVOICE RENEW DATE');

      billingMap = new Map();
      for (let i = headerRowIdx+1; i < rows.length; i++){
        const row = rows[i];
        const uname = row[uCol];
        if (!uname) continue;
        const key = String(uname).trim().toLowerCase();
        billingMap.set(key, {
          amount: amtCol>=0 ? row[amtCol] : null,
          renewDate: dateCol>=0 ? row[dateCol] : null
        });
      }
      document.getElementById('billingStatus').textContent = `✓ ${file.name} — ${billingMap.size} billed usernames matched`;
      document.getElementById('billingCard').classList.add('loaded');
      document.getElementById('billingReset').style.display = 'inline-flex';
      saveBillingToStorage(file.name);
      render();
    } catch(err){
      alert('Could not read this file: ' + err.message);
    }
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
});

document.getElementById('billingReset').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
  billingMap = null;
  document.getElementById('billingStatus').textContent = '';
  document.getElementById('billingCard').classList.remove('loaded');
  document.getElementById('billingInput').value = '';
  document.getElementById('billingReset').style.display = 'none';
  if (window.storage){ window.storage.delete('billing-data', false).catch(()=>{}); }
  render();
});

/* ---------------- Follow-up upload ---------------- */
document.getElementById('fuInput').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  const file = e.target.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (ev) => {
    try {
      const wb = XLSX.read(ev.target.result, { type:'array', cellDates:true });
      const sheet = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
      const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, { header:1, defval:null });
      const headerRowIdx = rows.findIndex(r => r.some(c => String(c||'').trim().toUpperCase() === 'USERNAME'));
      if (headerRowIdx === -1){ alert('USERNAME column not found in this sheet.'); return; }
      const header = rows[headerRowIdx].map(h => String(h||'').trim().toUpperCase());
      const uCol = header.indexOf('USERNAME');
      const branchCol = header.indexOf('BRANCH');
      const oltCol = header.indexOf('OLT');
      const daysCol = header.indexOf('DAYS REM');
      const typeCol = header.indexOf('TYPE');
      const followDateCol = header.indexOf('FOLLOW DATE');
      const followByCol = header.indexOf('FOLLOW BY');

      followupRows = [];
      for (let i = headerRowIdx+1; i < rows.length; i++){
        const row = rows[i];
        const uname = row[uCol];
        if (!uname) continue;
        followupRows.push({
          usernameKey: String(uname).trim().toLowerCase(),
          username: uname,
          branch: branchCol>=0 ? row[branchCol] : null,
          olt: oltCol>=0 ? row[oltCol] : null,
          daysRem: daysCol>=0 ? Number(row[daysCol]) : null,
          type: typeCol>=0 ? row[typeCol] : null,
          followDate: followDateCol>=0 ? row[followDateCol] : null,
          followBy: followByCol>=0 ? row[followByCol] : null
        });
      }
      document.getElementById('fuStatus').textContent = `✓ ${file.name} — ${followupRows.length} follow-up calls loaded`;
      document.getElementById('fuCard').classList.add('loaded');
      document.getElementById('fuReset').style.display = 'inline-flex';
      saveFollowupToStorage(file.name);
      render();
    } catch(err){
      alert('Could not read this file: ' + err.message);
    }
  };
  reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
});

document.getElementById('fuReset').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  e.preventDefault(); e.stopPropagation();
  followupRows = null;
  document.getElementById('fuStatus').textContent = '';
  document.getElementById('fuCard').classList.remove('loaded');
  document.getElementById('fuInput').value = '';
  document.getElementById('fuReset').style.display = 'none';
  if (window.storage){ window.storage.delete('followup-data', false).catch(()=>{}); }
  render();
});

document.getElementById('fuEmptyLink').addEventListener('click', () => document.getElementById('fuInput').click());

/* ---------------- Browser auto-save (persists uploads across reloads) ---------------- */
function saveRiskToStorage(fileName){
  if (!window.storage || !ALL_USERS) return;
  try {
    window.storage.set('risk-data', JSON.stringify({ fileName, users: ALL_USERS }), false).catch(()=>{});
  } catch(e){}
}
function saveBillingToStorage(fileName){
  if (!window.storage || !billingMap) return;
  try {
    window.storage.set('billing-data', JSON.stringify({ fileName, entries: Array.from(billingMap.entries()) }), false).catch(()=>{});
  } catch(e){}
}
function saveFollowupToStorage(fileName){
  if (!window.storage || !followupRows) return;
  try {
    window.storage.set('followup-data', JSON.stringify({ fileName, rows: followupRows }), false).catch(()=>{});
  } catch(e){}
}
async function restoreFromStorage(){
  if (!window.storage) return;
  try {
    const r = await window.storage.get('risk-data', false);
    if (r && r.value){
      const data = JSON.parse(r.value);
      if (data.users && data.users.length){
        ALL_USERS = data.users;
        document.getElementById('riskStatus').textContent = `✓ ${data.fileName} — ${ALL_USERS.length.toLocaleString()} users loaded (restored)`;
        document.getElementById('riskCard').classList.add('loaded');
        document.getElementById('riskReset').style.display = 'inline-flex';
        buildBranchOptions();
        buildOltOptions();
        renderHeader();
      }
    }
  } catch(e){}
  try {
    const b = await window.storage.get('billing-data', false);
    if (b && b.value){
      const data = JSON.parse(b.value);
      billingMap = new Map(data.entries);
      document.getElementById('billingStatus').textContent = `✓ ${data.fileName} — ${billingMap.size} billed usernames matched (restored)`;
      document.getElementById('billingCard').classList.add('loaded');
      document.getElementById('billingReset').style.display = 'inline-flex';
    }
  } catch(e){}
  try {
    const f = await window.storage.get('followup-data', false);
    if (f && f.value){
      const data = JSON.parse(f.value);
      followupRows = data.rows;
      document.getElementById('fuStatus').textContent = `✓ ${data.fileName} — ${followupRows.length} follow-up calls loaded (restored)`;
      document.getElementById('fuCard').classList.add('loaded');
      document.getElementById('fuReset').style.display = 'inline-flex';
    }
  } catch(e){}
  render();
}

function groupFollowupsByOlt(filtered){
  if (!followupRows) return {};
  const scopeKeys = new Map(filtered.map(u => [u.usernameKey, u]));
  const out = {};
  followupRows.forEach(f => {
    const u = scopeKeys.get(f.usernameKey);
    if (!u) return;
    const key = u.branch + '||' + u.olt;
    out[key] = (out[key]||0) + 1;
  });
  return out;
}

function bucketOf(daysRem){
  if (daysRem === null || isNaN(daysRem)) return 'unknown';
  if (daysRem > 0) return 'pre';
  const overdue = Math.abs(daysRem);
  if (overdue <= 1) return 'd1';
  if (overdue === 2) return 'd2';
  if (overdue === 3) return 'd3';
  return 'd3plus';
}

function dateKey(v){
  if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return null;
  let d = v instanceof Date ? v : new Date(v);
  if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return null;
  return d.toISOString().slice(0,10); // YYYY-MM-DD, sortable
}
function dateLabel(key){
  const d = new Date(key + 'T00:00:00Z');
  return d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day:'2-digit', month:'short', timeZone:'UTC' });
}

function renderFollowup(filtered){
  const empty = document.getElementById('fuEmpty');
  const content = document.getElementById('fuContent');
  if (!followupRows){ empty.style.display='block'; content.style.display='none'; return; }
  empty.style.display='none'; content.style.display='block';

  // Only follow-ups that belong to a high-risk username currently in scope
  const scopeKeys = new Map(filtered.map(u => [u.usernameKey, u]));
  const rows = followupRows.filter(f => scopeKeys.has(f.usernameKey));

  const uniqueUsers = new Set(rows.map(r => r.usernameKey)).size;
  const dateSet = new Set();
  rows.forEach(f => { const k = dateKey(f.followDate); if (k) f._dk = k, dateSet.add(k); });
  const dates = Array.from(dateSet).sort();
  const oltSet = new Set(rows.map(f => (scopeKeys.get(f.usernameKey)||{}).olt || f.olt).filter(Boolean));

  document.getElementById('fuTotal').textContent = rows.length.toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById('fuUsers').textContent = uniqueUsers.toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById('fuDays').textContent = dates.length;
  document.getElementById('fuOlts').textContent = oltSet.size;
  document.getElementById('fuAvgDay').textContent = dates.length ? (rows.length/dates.length).toFixed(1) : '0';

  // Pivot: rows = Branch+OLT (of the matched high-risk user, authoritative source), columns = dates
  const groups = {};
  rows.forEach(f => {
    const u = scopeKeys.get(f.usernameKey);
    const branch = (u && u.branch) || f.branch || '—';
    const olt = (u && u.olt) || f.olt || '—';
    const key = branch + '||' + olt;
    if (!groups[key]) groups[key] = { branch, olt, byDate:{}, total:0 };
    if (f._dk){ groups[key].byDate[f._dk] = (groups[key].byDate[f._dk]||0) + 1; }
    groups[key].total++;
  });
  const gArr = Object.values(groups).sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total);

  const thead = document.getElementById('fuTableHead');
  thead.innerHTML = `<tr>
      <th>Branch</th><th>OLT</th>
      ${dates.map(d => `<th>${dateLabel(d)}</th>`).join('')}
      <th>Total</th>
    </tr>`;

  const tbody = document.getElementById('fuTableBody');
  tbody.innerHTML = gArr.map(g => `
    <tr>
      <td style="color:var(--text-dim)">${g.branch}</td>
      <td><b>${g.olt}</b></td>
      ${dates.map(d => {
        const c = g.byDate[d] || 0;
        return `<td>${c > 0 ? c : '<span style="color:var(--text-faint)">–</span>'}</td>`;
      }).join('')}
      <td>${g.total}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('') || '';

  if (!gArr.length){
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="${dates.length+3}" style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-faint);padding:20px;">No follow-up calls matched to a high-risk user in this scope.</td></tr>`;
  }
}

/* ---------------- Unpaid & expired list ---------------- */
let unpaidSortKey = 'daysOverdue', unpaidSortDir = -1;
let unpaidSearchTerm = '';
let unpaidOltFilterVal = '__ALL__';
let unpaidFuFilterVal = '__ALL__';
let unpaidBillFilterVal = '__ALL__';

function followupCountFor(usernameKey){
  if (!followupRows) return null;
  return followupRows.filter(f => f.usernameKey === usernameKey).length;
}

function computeUnpaidList(filtered){
  let expired = filtered.filter(u => u.daysRemaining !== null && u.daysRemaining <= 0);
  return expired.map(u => ({
    username: u.username, branch: u.branch, olt: u.olt, riskScore: u.riskScore,
    daysOverdue: Math.abs(u.daysRemaining), expiryDate: u.expiryDate, revenue: u.revenue,
    type: u.type, followUps: followupCountFor(u.usernameKey),
    billed: billingMap ? billingMap.has(u.usernameKey) : null
  }));
}

function syncUnpaidOltOptions(rows){
  const sel = document.getElementById('unpaidOltFilter');
  const olts = Array.from(new Set(rows.map(r => r.olt).filter(Boolean))).sort();
  const current = unpaidOltFilterVal;
  sel.innerHTML = '<option value="__ALL__">All OLTs</option>' + olts.map(o => `<option value="${o}">${o}</option>`).join('');
  sel.value = olts.includes(current) ? current : '__ALL__';
  unpaidOltFilterVal = sel.value;
}

function renderUnpaidList(filtered){
  let rows = computeUnpaidList(filtered);

  syncUnpaidOltOptions(rows);

  if (unpaidOltFilterVal !== '__ALL__'){
    rows = rows.filter(r => r.olt === unpaidOltFilterVal);
  }
  if (unpaidFuFilterVal !== '__ALL__'){
    rows = rows.filter(r => unpaidFuFilterVal === 'yes' ? (r.followUps !== null && r.followUps > 0) : (r.followUps !== null && r.followUps === 0));
  }
  if (unpaidBillFilterVal !== '__ALL__'){
    rows = rows.filter(r => unpaidBillFilterVal === 'yes' ? r.billed === true : r.billed === false);
  }

  if (unpaidSearchTerm){
    const t = unpaidSearchTerm.toLowerCase();
    rows = rows.filter(r => (r.username||'').toLowerCase().includes(t) || (r.branch||'').toLowerCase().includes(t) || (r.olt||'').toLowerCase().includes(t));
  }

  rows.sort((a,b) => {
    const va = a[unpaidSortKey], vb = b[unpaidSortKey];
    if (va === null || va === undefined) return 1; if (vb === null || vb === undefined) return -1;
    if (typeof va === 'string') return va.localeCompare(vb) * unpaidSortDir;
    return (va - vb) * unpaidSortDir;
  });

  document.getElementById('unpaidTableBody').innerHTML = rows.map(r => `
    <tr>
      <td style="color:var(--text)">${r.username}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--text-dim)">${r.branch}</td>
      <td>${r.olt}</td>
      <td>${riskPill(r.riskScore||0)}</td>
      <td><span class="pill red">${r.daysOverdue}d</span></td>
      <td style="color:var(--text-dim)">${r.expiryDate||'—'}</td>
      <td>${r.revenue!=null ? 'NPR '+r.revenue.toLocaleString(undefined,{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}) : '—'}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--text-dim)">${r.type||'—'}</td>
      <td>${r.billed===null ? '<span style="color:var(--text-faint)">—</span>' : (r.billed ? '<span class="pill green">Yes</span>' : '<span class="pill red">No</span>')}</td>
      <td>${r.followUps===null ? '<span style="color:var(--text-faint)">—</span>' : (r.followUps>0 ? `<span class="pill amber">${r.followUps}</span>` : '<span class="pill red">0</span>')}</td>
    </tr>
  `).join('') || `<tr><td colspan="10" style="text-align:center;color:var(--text-faint);padding:26px;">No expired &amp; unpaid users in this scope 🎉</td></tr>`;

  document.getElementById('unpaidCount').textContent = `${rows.length.toLocaleString()} user(s) shown`;
  window.__unpaidExportRows = rows;
}

document.querySelectorAll('#unpaidTable thead th').forEach(th => {
  th.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const key = th.dataset.key;
    if (unpaidSortKey === key) unpaidSortDir *= -1; else { unpaidSortKey = key; unpaidSortDir = -1; }
    render();
  });
});
document.getElementById('unpaidSearch').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
  unpaidSearchTerm = e.target.value; render();
});
document.getElementById('unpaidOltFilter').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  unpaidOltFilterVal = e.target.value; render();
});
document.getElementById('unpaidFuFilter').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  unpaidFuFilterVal = e.target.value; render();
});
document.getElementById('unpaidBillFilter').addEventListener('change', (e) => {
  unpaidBillFilterVal = e.target.value; render();
});

document.getElementById('exportBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const rows = window.__unpaidExportRows || [];
  const exportData = rows.map(r => ({
    'Username': r.username, 'Branch': r.branch, 'OLT': r.olt, 'Risk Score': r.riskScore,
    'Days Overdue': r.daysOverdue, 'Expiry Date': r.expiryDate,
    'Forecast Revenue (NPR)': r.revenue, 'Type': r.type,
    'Billed': r.billed === null ? 'N/A (upload billing sheet)' : (r.billed ? 'Yes' : 'No'),
    'Follow-ups Logged': r.followUps === null ? 'N/A (upload CFU sheet)' : r.followUps
  }));
  const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(exportData);
  ws['!cols'] = [{wch:22},{wch:18},{wch:10},{wch:10},{wch:13},{wch:14},{wch:20},{wch:14},{wch:12},{wch:18}];
  const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, 'Expired Unpaid');
  const branch = branchSel.value === '__ALL__' ? 'AllBranches' : branchSel.value;
  const olt = oltSel.value === '__ALL__' ? 'AllOLTs' : oltSel.value;
  XLSX.writeFile(wb, `Expired_Unpaid_${branch}_${olt}.xlsx`);
});

/* ---------------- Filter summary tag ---------------- */
function renderFilterSummary(filtered){
  const b = branchSel.value === '__ALL__' ? 'All branches' : branchSel.value;
  const o = oltSel.value === '__ALL__' ? 'all OLTs' : oltSel.value;
  document.getElementById('filterSummary').innerHTML = `<b>${b}</b> · ${o} — ${filtered.length.toLocaleString()} users in view`;
}

/* ---------------- Master render ---------------- */
function render(){
  const filtered = currentFiltered();
  renderOverview(filtered);
  renderOltRawTable(filtered);
  renderOltTable(filtered);
  renderFollowup(filtered);
  renderUnpaidList(filtered);
  renderFilterSummary(filtered);
}

renderHeader();
buildBranchOptions();
buildOltOptions();
render();
restoreFromStorage();

</script>
</body>
</html>
